2026-11-06T13:53:36,629  INFO ScoringConfigGroup$ScoringParameterSet:1630 NO OPENING OR CLOSING TIMES DEFINED!

There is no activity type that has an opening *and* closing time (or opening time and late penalty) defined.
This usually means that the activity chains can be shifted by an arbitrary
number of hours without having an effect on the score of the plans, and thus
resulting in wrong results / traffic patterns.
If you are using MATSim without time adaptation, you can ignore this warning.

2026-11-06T13:58:31,587  INFO ScoringConfigGroup$ScoringParameterSet:1630 NO OPENING OR CLOSING TIMES DEFINED!

There is no activity type that has an opening *and* closing time (or opening time and late penalty) defined.

2026-11-06T13:59:33,256  INFO ScoringConfigGroup$ScoringParameterSet:1630 NO OPENING OR CLOSING TIMES DEFINED!

There is no activity type that has an opening *and* closing time (or opening time and late penalty) defined.
This usually means that the activity chains can be shifted by an arbitrary
number of hours without having an effect on the score of the plans, and thus
resulting in wrong results / traffic patterns.
If you are using MATSim without time adaptation, you can ignore this warning.

resulting in wrong results / traffic patterns.
If you are using MATSim without time adaptation, you can ignore this warning.

2026-11-06T14:02:40,334  INFO ScoringConfigGroup$ScoringParameterSet:1630 NO OPENING OR CLOSING TIMES DEFINED!

There is no activity type that has an opening *and* closing time (or opening time and late penalty) defined.
This usually means that the activity chains can be shifted by an arbitrary
number of hours without having an effect on the score of the plans, and thus
resulting in wrong results / traffic patterns.
If you are using MATSim without time adaptation, you can ignore this warning.
